                                             ARTICLE 3
                                             PURPOSE

                Section 3.1. Purpose and Business: The purpose and nature of the business
 to be conducted by the Company is to acquire, hold, own, develop, redevelop, construct, improve,
 maintain, operate, manage, sell, lease, transfer, finance, refinance, encumber, convey, exchange
 and otherwise dispose of or deal with the Property, and to engage in such other ancillary activities
 as shall be necessary or desirable to effectuate the foregoing purposes, all in accordance with the
 terms of this Agreement. The Company and its Manager shall have all powers necessary or
 desirable to accomplish the purposes enumerated. The Company shall not engage in any other
 business or acquire any other property or provide financial support of any kind to any other
 property without the consent of all the Members.

                Section 3.2. Powers:        In connection with the foregoing, but subject to all of
 the terms, covenants, conditions and limitations contained in this Agreement and any other
 agreement entered into by the Company, the Company shall have full power and authority to enter
 into, perform and carry out contracts of any kind, to borrow money and to issue evidences of
 indebtedness, whether or not secured by mortgage, trust deed, pledge or other Lien and to do any
 and all acts and things necessary, appropriate, proper, advisable, incidental to or convenient for
 the furtherance and accomplishment of the purposes and business described herein and for the
 protection and benefit of the Company.

                                  ARTICLE 4
              CAPITAL CONTRIBUTIONS; CAPITAL ACCOUNTS; FINANCING

                Section 4.1. Initial Capital Contributions of the Members: As of the date hereof,
 each Initial Member has made, or is deemed to have made, the initial Capital Contribution to the
 Company set forth next to its name on Schedule A.

                 Section 4.2. Additional Capital Contributions.

        (a)    In the event that the Company requires additional capital (after having exhausted the
funds available to it during the relevant period) to pay expenses reasonably necessary for the
maintenance of its assets or to meet operating or administration expenses of the Company, including
contingent liabilities, lawsuits or governmental investigations, or to make any capital expenditures
or other disbursements to third parties that the Manager reasonably determines in good faith and in
 00311432.4

===== PAGE 242 =====
the best interests of the Company are necessary to preserve Company’s value or to protect it from
waste (collectively, the “Necessary Expenses”), then to the extent the funds for such Necessary
Expenses cannot reasonably be expected to be obtained from existing funds, a commercial loan or
operating activities during the relevant period, only the Manager may send a notice to the Members
(the “Call Notice”) that (i) sets forth the reasons supporting such additional capital call; (ii) the
aggregate amount of such additional capital call; (iii) the amount of each Member’s pro rata share
of the Necessary Expenses, determined by reference to the ratio that each Member’s respective
percentage interest bears to the aggregate of all the Members’ percentage interests; and (iv) the date
by which such additional capital must be paid to the Company, which date shall be not less than 30
days following the date of delivery of such notice. Within 15 days of the date of receipt of such
notice, each Member shall notify the Manager whether such Member intends to contribute the
additional capital pursuant to the Call Notice in accordance with their respective Economic Interests
(any additional capital contributed after the date hereof solely for purposes of funding any Necessary
Expenses are referred to herein as “Additional Capital”). A Member that fails to contribute its pro
rata share of the Additional Capital prior to the expiration of the period specified in the Call Notice
is referred to herein as a “Non-Contributing Member” and a party that actually funded its required
contribution (a “Funded Contribution”) is referred to herein as a “Contributing Member”. The
Manager shall give prompt notice of such failure and the amount of the contribution not funded to
the Company (such amount is hereinafter referred to as the “Failed Contribution”) and within 10
Business Days after the date of such notice, the Contributing Members shall notify the Manager of
their willingness to make an advance to fund all or a portion of the Failed Contribution (a “Make-Up
Advance”). The Contributing Members shall be entitled to fund the Failed Contributions pro rata.
The Contributing Members that fund Make-Up Advances on behalf of Non-Contributing Members
are referred to as “Make-Up Members”. The Manager shall send a notice to each Member
immediately following the expiration of such ten-day period setting forth the Funded Contributions
and the related Contributing Members and the Make-Up Advances and the related Make-Up
Members. Upon receipt of the Additional Capital, the Manager shall amend Schedule A to reflect a
pro rata increase in the Capital Contributions of the Members based upon the aggregate amount of
the Additional Capital actually received pursuant to the capital call. The Economic Interest of each
Member shall not change as a result of the Additional Capital. All Make-Up Advances shall be
treated as an advance by the related Make-Up Member to the Non-Contributing Member to fund a
capital contribution by the Non-Contributing Member to the Company, which advance shall be
repaid in accordance with the provisions of this Subsection. All distributions payable in accordance
with this section to a Non-Contributing Member shall be paid to all of the contributing members, pro
rata, the “Make-Up” Members that made a Make-Up Advance on behalf of such Non-Contributing
Member pro rata until such time as such Make-Up Members have received an amount equal to a rate
of return on its Make-Up Advance equal to 12% per annum, compounded annually, from the date
such Make-Up Advance was made until repaid, and the Make-Up Advance; provided, however, that
for a period of fourteen (14) days following the date that the Make-Up Advance is paid to the
Company, the Non-Contributing Member shall be entitled to elect to pay the Make-Up Members the
full Make-Up Advance together with an amount equal to 12% per annum, compounded annually,
from the date such Make-Up Advance was made, in which case the Make-Up Advance shall be
deemed to have been a Funded Contribution made by the Non-Contributing Member. All member
loans to the Company will be al pi heter Iska and in accordance with Halacha.

                 (b) If after attempting to receive additional funds pursuant to paragraphs (a),
additional funding is needed in connection with operation or development of the Project, then
Manager may (i) attempt to secure mezzanine financing for the Property on such terms as may be
available in the marketplace, and/or (ii) attempt to attract additional equity investors. If Manager
 00311432.4

===== PAGE 243 =====
anticipates that the admittance of new equity investors or the mezzanine financing will cause the
Percentage Interests of the Members to be diluted, then the Members shall be given a preemptive
right of first offer with respect to such equity and/or mezzanine financing opportunity (in either
case an “Equity Offering”) as follows:

                          (i)     Manager shall circulate a proposed term sheet to all of the Members
          outlining the material economic terms of the Equity Offering (the “Initial Offer”) and
          specifically the pricing for the sale of any Company Interests to third party investors. Each
          of the Members shall have fifteen (15) days to elect to invest additional funds in the
          Company on the terms and conditions of the Initial Offer. If a Member does not respond to
          the Initial Offer within the fifteen (15) day period, such Member shall be deemed to have
          elected not to participate in the Equity Offering. If more than one Member elects to
          participate in the Equity Offering, then such participation shall be pro- rata based on each
          Member’s Percentage Interest or such other division as agreed upon by the participating
          Members.

                         (ii)    If none of the Members elect to participate in the Equity Offering,
          or the participation by the Members does not raise all of the equity required by the
          Company, then Manager, for a period of one hundred eighty (180) days after the date of
          the Initial Offer (the “Offering Period”) may open the Equity Offering to third party
          investors and shall solicit additional equity investments in the Company upon terms and
          conditions no more favorable to the potential investors than those set forth in the Initial
          Offer.

              (iii)           If the Manager accepts an offer by a new investor during the
Offering Period, that is no more favorable to the investor than the Initial Offer, then, subject to
compliance with Section 10.4 hereof, the Manager may admit any third party investors to the
Company as an Additional Member upon such terms as set forth in the new investor’s offer. In
connection with the sale of Company Interests, the new investors may be issued a Percentage Interest
in the Company. If a Percentage Interest is issued to a new investor, then the Manager’s and the Members'
Percentage Interests shall be diluted pro-rata by the interest of each new Member, but to no greater extent
than proposed in the Initial Offer.

                 Section 4.3. No Third Party Beneficiary:           No creditor or other third
 party having dealings with the Company shall have the right to enforce the right or obligation of
 any Member to make Capital Contributions or loans or to pursue any other right or remedy under
 this Agreement or at law or in equity, it being understood and agreed that the provisions of this
 Agreement shall be solely for the benefit of, and may be enforced solely by, the parties hereto and
 their respective successors and assigns.

               Section 4.4 Capital Accounts: A. A separate Capital Account shall be established
 and maintained for each Economic Interest Holder on the books and records of the Company in
 accordance with Regulations Section 1.704-1(b). Each Economic Interest Holder’s Capital
 Account shall be credited with such Economic Interest Holder’s initial Capital Contribution, as
 provided in Section 4.1, and shall be adjusted from time to time as follows:

               (a)     The Capital Account shall be increased by (i) the Economic Interest
 Holder’s Capital Contributions in cash, (ii) the fair market value of any property (net of liabilities
 secured by such contributed property that the Company is considered to assume or take subject to
 00311432.4

===== PAGE 244 =====
under Code Section 752) contributed to the capital of the Company by the Economic Interest
Holder, (iii) the amount of any Net Income allocated to the Economic Interest Holder under Section
5.1 hereof, (iv) the Economic Interest Holder’s share of any separately allocated items of income
or gain, (v) the amount of any Company liabilities that such Economic Interest Holder has agreed
to pay, and (iv) liabilities which are secured by any asset distributed to such Economic Interest
Holder and which are assumed by such Economic Interest Holder (whether on a recourse or non-
recourse basis); and

                  (b)    The Capital Account shall be decreased by (i) the amount of cash and the
fair market value of any Company property (net of liabilities secured by such contributed property
that the Economic Interest Holder is considered to assume or take subject to under Code Section
752) distributed to such Economic Interest Holder pursuant to this Agreement, (ii) the amount of
Net Losses allocated to the Economic Interest Holder under Section 5.1 hereof, (iii) the Economic
Interest Holder’s share of any separately allocated items of expenses or losses, (iv) the amount of
any liabilities of such Economic Interest Holder that the Company has agreed to pay and (iv) any
liability that is secured by any asset contributed by such Economic Interest Holder to the Company
(and that is assumed by the Company, whether on a recourse or non- recourse basis).

                B.      The foregoing provisions of this Section 4.4 and the other provisions of this
Agreement related to the maintenance of Capital Accounts are intended to comply with
Regulations Section 1.704-1(b), and shall be interpreted and applied in a manner consistent with
such Regulations. To the extent that such provisions are inconsistent with such Regulations or are
incomplete with respect thereto, Capital Accounts shall be maintained in accordance with such
Regulations.
                Section 4.5. No Interest; No Return of Adjusted Contributions: No Economic
Interest Holder shall be entitled to interest on its Capital Account. Except as provided herein or by
law, no Economic Interest Holder shall have any right to demand or receive the return of its
Adjusted Contributions from the Company. No Economic Interest Holder shall be required to
return to the Company any distribution properly made to it in accordance with the terms of this
Agreement. Each Economic Interest Holder shall look solely to the assets of the Company for the
return of its Adjusted Contributions. No Manager or Member shall be personally liable for a return
of any Adjusted Contributions of any Economic Interest Holder. No Economic Interest Holder
shall have the right or power to demand or receive property other than cash from the Company.
Each Economic Interest Holder hereby waives any right to partition of the Company property. No
Economic Interest Holder shall have any obligation to pay to the Company or to any other Member
an amount equal to the negative balance in such Member’s Capital Account.




                                    ARTICLE 5
                         ALLOCATION OF PROFITS AND LOSSES

                Section 5.1. Allocation of Net Income and Net Loss: A. Net Income. Except
as otherwise provided in this Section 5.1, Net Income (or items thereof) of the Company for any
fiscal year or other applicable period shall be allocated to the Economic Interest Holders first in
accordance with any prior allocation of Net Losses, other than Nonrecourse Deductions and
Member Nonrecourse Deductions, pro rata, until each Member has been allocated an amount of
Net Income pursuant to this clause equal to the cumulative amount of Net Losses, other than
00311432.4

===== PAGE 245 =====
Nonrecourse Deductions and Member Nonrecourse Deductions, that have been allocated to such
Economic Interest Holder, and thereafter to the Economic Interest Holders in accordance with the
amount of cumulative distributions that have been made to such Economic Interest Holder, or, to
the extent such Net Income exceeds Available Cash, the distributions that would be made to such
Economic Interest Holder if the Company had an amount of Available Cash equal to such Net
Income.

                               B.    Net Loss.      Except as otherwise provided in this Article 5,
Net Loss (or items thereof) of the Company for each fiscal year or other applicable period shall be
allocated to the Economic Interest Holders first, so as to offset prior allocations of Net Income in
the reverse order in which such Net Income was allocated, and then in accordance with the positive
Capital Account balances of the Economic Interest Holders, pro rata, until such Capital Accounts
have been reduced to zero, and thereafter in accordance with Percentage Interests.

                Section 5.2. Special Allocations: Notwithstanding any provisions of Section
5.1 hereof, the following special allocations shall be made:

                               (a)    Minimum Gain Chargeback (Nonrecourse Liabilities).
Except as otherwise provided in Section 1.704-2(f) of the Regulations, if there is a net decrease in
Company Minimum Gain for any Company fiscal year, each Economic Interest Holder shall be
specially allocated items of Company income and gain for such year (and, if necessary, subsequent
years) in an amount equal to such Economic Interest Holder’s share of the net decrease in Company
Minimum Gain to the extent required by Regulations Section 1.704-2(f). The items to be so
allocated shall be determined in accordance with Sections 1.704-2(f) and (i) of the Regulations.
This Section 5.2(a) is intended to comply with the minimum gain chargeback requirement in said
section of the Regulations and shall be interpreted consistently therewith. Allocations pursuant to
this Section 5.2(a) shall be made in proportion to the respective amounts required to be allocated
to each Economic Interest Holder pursuant hereto.

                              (b)    Member Minimum Gain Chargeback.                   Except     as
otherwise provided in Section 1.704-2(i)(4) of the Regulations, if there is a net decrease in Member
Minimum Gain attributable to a Member Nonrecourse Debt during any fiscal year, each Economic
Interest Holder who has a share of the Member Minimum Gain attributable to such Member
Nonrecourse Debt, determined in accordance with Section 1.704-2(i)(5) of the Regulations, shall
be specially allocated items of Company income and gain for such year (and, if necessary,
subsequent years) in an amount equal to that Economic Interest Holder’s share of the net decrease
in the Member Minimum Gain attributable to such Member Nonrecourse Debt to the extent and
in the manner required by Section 1.704-2(i) of the Regulations. The items to be allocated shall
be determined in accordance with Section 1.704-2(i)(4) and (j)(2) of the Regulations. This Section
5.2(b) is intended to comply with the minimum gain chargeback requirement with respect to
member Nonrecourse Debt contained in said section of the Regulations and shall be interpreted
consistently therewith. Allocations pursuant to this Section 5.2(b) shall be made in proportion to
the respective amounts required to be allocated to each Economic Interest Holder pursuant hereto.

                                (c)     Qualified Income Offset. In the event an Economic Interest
Holder unexpectedly receives any adjustments, allocations or distributions described in Section
1.704-1(b)(2)(ii)(d)(4), (5) or (6) of the Regulations, and such Economic Interest Holder has an
Adjusted Capital Account Deficit, items of Company income (including gross income) and gain
shall be specially allocated to such Economic Interest Holder in an amount and manner sufficient
00311432.4

===== PAGE 246 =====
to eliminate the Adjusted Capital Account Deficit as quickly as possible as required by the
Regulations. This Section 5.2(c) is intended to constitute a “qualified income offset” under Section
1.704-1(b)(2)(ii)(d) of the Regulations and shall be interpreted consistently therewith.

                             (d)     Special Limitation. Notwithstanding the foregoing, if any
special allocation otherwise required pursuant to Section 5.2(a) or 5.2(c) would cause the
Company’s allocations to violate Section 514(a)(9)(B)(vi) of the Code, then such special allocation
shall not be made.

                               (e)    Nonrecourse Deductions. Nonrecourse Deductions for any
fiscal year or other applicable period shall be allocated to the Members in accordance with their
respective Percentage Interests.
                             (f)     Member Nonrecourse Deductions. Member Nonrecourse
Deductions for any fiscal year or other applicable period with respect to a Member Nonrecourse
Debt shall be specially allocated to the Member that bears the economic risk of loss for such
Member Nonrecourse Debt (as determined under Sections 1.704-2(b)(4) and 1.704-2(i)(1) of the
Regulations).

                                 (g)     Intent of Allocations. (i) The parties intend that the
allocation provisions of this Article 5 shall result in Capital Account balances of the Economic
Interest Holder as of the end of each fiscal year, that reflect, after taking into account all allocations
and distributions for the fiscal year, the amounts that would be distributed to each Member if (a)
the Company were to sell all of its assets for their Gross Asset Value and (b) were to liquidate and
distribute the net proceeds of such sale in accordance with Section 6.1 hereof. To the extent that
such Capital Account balances do not so reflect the provisions of such Section 6.1, Net Income
and Net Loss for the fiscal year (and in the year in which the Company disposes of all or
substantially all of its assets, items thereof) shall be allocated to cause such Capital Accounts to
reflect the provisions of Section 6.1 to the greatest extent possible.

                                (h)      Section 754 Adjustment. To the extent an adjustment to
the adjusted tax basis of any asset of the Company pursuant to Section 734(b) of the Code or
Section 743(b) of the Code is required, pursuant to Section 1.704-1(b)(2)(iv)(m) of the
Regulations, to be taken into account in determining Capital Accounts, the amount of such
adjustment to the Capital Accounts shall be treated as an item of gain (if the adjustment increases
the basis of the asset) or loss (if the adjustments decreases such basis) and such gain or loss shall
be specially allocated among the Members in a manner consistent with the manner in which each
of their respective Capital Accounts are required to be adjusted pursuant to such section of the
Regulations.

The foregoing provisions and the other provisions of Article 4 and this Article 5 relating to the
maintenance of Capital Accounts and allocations of Net Income and Net Loss (collectively the
“Allocations Provisions”) are intended to comply with the Code Section 514(c)(9)(E) and the
Treasury Regulations thereunder (the “Fractions Rule”) and Code 704(b) and the Treasury
Regulations thereunder, and shall be interpreted and applied in a manner consistent with such
Regulations. The Allocation Provisions are deemed modified, with effect from the date of this
Agreement, to the extent necessary to comply with the Fractions Rule. Without limiting the
foregoing, any allocation for a particular year pursuant to the Allocation Provisions which would
violate the requirements of Sections 704(b) and 514(c)(9)(E) of the Code shall not be made and
there shall instead be made allocations for such year consistent with such requirements and
00311432.4

===== PAGE 247 =====
deviating as little as possible from the allocations generally provided herein, as determined by the
Tax Matters Member.

                Section 5.3.    Tax Allocations:

                                A.    Items of Income or Loss.    Except as is otherwise provided
in this Article 5, an allocation of Company Net Income or Net Loss to an Economic Interest Holder
shall be treated as an allocation to such Economic Interest Holder of the same share of each item
of income, gain, loss, deduction and item of tax exempt income or Section 705(a)(2)(B)
expenditure (or any item treated as such expenditure pursuant to Regulations Section 1.704-
1(b)(2)(iv)(i) that is taken into account in computing Net Income of Net Loss.
                              B.      Section 1245/1250 Recapture.         In any portion of gain
from the sale of Company assets is treated as gain which is ordinary income by virtue of the
application of Code Sections 1245 or 1250 (“Affected Gain”), then such Affected Gain shall be
allocated among the Economic Interest Holders in the same proportion that the depreciation and
amortization deductions giving rise to the Affected Gain were allocated. This Section 5.3 shall not
alter the amount of Net Income (or items thereof) allocated among the Economic Interest Holders,
but merely the character of such Net Income (or items thereof). For purposes hereof, in order to
determine the proportionate allocations of depreciation and amortization deductions for each fiscal
year or other applicable period, such deductions shall be deemed allocated on the same basis as
Net Income and Net Loss for such respective period.

                             C.      Built-in Gain. The Company shall use any permissible
method contained in the Regulations promulgated under Section 704(c) of the Code selected by
the Tax Matters Member, in its sole discretion, to take into account any variation between the
adjusted basis of property contributed to the Company or the property being revalued by the
Company and the fair market value of such asset as of the time of the contribution or revaluation.
Each Economic Interest Holder hereby agrees to report income, gain, loss and deduction on such
Economic Interest Holder’s federal income tax return in a manner consistent with the method used
by the Company.

                                           ARTICLE 6
                                         DISTRIBUTIONS

                Section 6.1.    Distributions:

                    A.     Distributions of Available Cash. The Manager shall distribute
Available Cash among the Members in accordance with the following order of priority:

        All distributions will go, pro rata, to the membership interests.

                      B.      Distributions of Capital Proceeds. Capital Proceeds remaining
after the payment of any debts and liabilities of the Company due and payable at such time and
the establishment of any reserves which the Manager determines, in its reasonable discretion, to
be necessary to provide for any contingent or unforeseen liabilities or obligations of the
Company, shall be distributed as follows:

        All distributions will go, pro rata, to pay the Members on their Unreturned Initial Capital
00311432.4

===== PAGE 248 =====
         Contributions until their capital accounts are zero.

         Then, once the Members who had invested money with the Company have had their capital
         account zeroed out distributions shall be made pro rata to the membership interests.


                  Section 6.2. Withholding: With respect to any withholding tax or other similar tax
 liability or obligation to which the Company may be subject as a result of any act or status of any
 Economic Interest Holders or to which the Company becomes subject with respect to any
 Company Interest, the Company shall have the right to withhold amounts of Available Cash
 distributable to such Economic Interest Holders or with respect to such Company Interest, to the
 extent of the amount of such withholding tax or other similar tax liability or obligation. Any
 amount so withheld shall constitute a distribution made to the Economic Interest Holders on whose
 account the amount was withheld. Where the Available Cash distributable to such Economic
 Interest Holders is less than the amount of the Company’s withholding obligation with respect to
 such Economic Interest Holders, such Economic Interest Holders shall advance to the Company
 cash in an amount equal to the excess of the Company’s withholding obligation over the Available
 Cash distributable to such Economic Interest Holders. Any such advance by an Economic Interest
 Holders shall not be treated as a loan or contribution by the Economic Interest Holder to the
 Company, and the Economic Interest Holders shall not have any right to recover such advance
 from the Company or any of its Members.

                                    ARTICLE 7
                       MANAGEMENT AND OPERATIONS OF BUSINESS

                 Section 7.1.    Management:

                 (a)     The business affairs, decisions and management of the Company shall be
managed by a majority vote of the Managers, acting separately or alone, shall have the absolute
right to act for and bind the Company in all matters including, but not limited to, without
limitation: banking, institute any legal proceedings in the name of the Company; settle or defend
any legal proceedings or confess a judgment against the Company or any Company assets; cause
the Company to acquire or sell any or all of its personal property in the ordinary course of
business; agree to the sale, merger, or recapitalization of the Company, or the issuance of any
additional Company Interest or Percentage Interest in the Company if required as per this
agreement; and/or settle, on behalf of the Company, any suit, proceeding or arbitration before any
court or arbitrator or governmental agency. Notwithstanding the foregoing. The Manager shall
require a majority vote of the members if it wishes to contract with any outside company above
market rate. The Members shall have no power to remove Manager or to designate an additional or
a successor Manager, unless the Manager (i) resigns or is no longer able to fulfill his duties
hereunder because of his death or incapacity, (ii) commits a fraud, acts with gross negligence or
makes an intentional material misrepresentation in connection with the Members’ investment in
the Company, or (iii) misappropriates Company Funds; and, with respect to (ii) and (iii), any
dispute thereof shall be decided by expedited arbitration in accordance with the terms Section
10.5. In the event a new Manager needs to be appointed, the Members shall elect a replacement
Manager by a vote of consisting of sixty percent (60%) of the Percentage Interests of all the
Members

                 (b)     Each Member hereby approves the Management Agreement, dated as of the
 00311432.4

===== PAGE 249 =====
date hereof (the “Initial Management Agreement”) between the Company and GREEN TREE
HEALTHCARE 2 LLC (the “Initial Manager”), who shall be in charge of administering the day
to day operations of the facility and property, which Initial Management Agreement shall provide,
among other things, that the Company shall pay the manager a maximum fee equal to five percent
(5%) of gross receivables of the Company as compensation. The Managers may remove the Initial
Manager if the Initial Manager: (i) commits fraud or willful negligence; (ii) defaults in any material
way under the terms of their management agreement; (iii) defaults under the Loan Documents, and
said default remains uncured beyond any cure periods or is not waived by the Lender; or (iv) does
not maintain the financial coverage ratios found in the Loan Documents for 2 consecutive quarters.
Notwithstanding the foregoing, and absent fraud or willful misconduct, the Parties agree to
reasonably work together with the Initial Manager to cure any non-material defaults in a timely
manner.

                 (c)     Notwithstanding anything contained in 7.1(a), for as long as the members
initial capital contributions and preferred returns have not been repaid to the members, any sale of
the real property will require a 60% vote of the membership unless: (i) the property has been listed
with a licensed broker for a minimum of thirty (30) days and the sale price would be sufficient to
allow for the return of the members initial capital contributions and any outstanding preferred
returns not already paid to the member, if same has not already been previously returned to the
members or (ii) the loan is in jeopardy of being in default under the terms of the loan and security
agreement. Under this subparagraph (ii) the members will be given 5 days-notice of any loan
defaults, if same can be remedied with money, within which to escrow 12 months of mortgage
payments.

              Section 7.2 Intentionally Omitted

                Section 7.3 Meetings and Voting. A meeting of the Members may be called at any
time by the Manager on not less than five (5) business days prior notice. Meetings of Members
shall be held during normal business hours at the Company’s principal place of business or at any
other place in Rhode Island designated by the manager. The notice calling the meeting shall state
the place, date, hour, and purpose of the meeting. Notwithstanding the foregoing provisions, each
Member who is entitled to notice waives notice if before or after the meeting the Member signs a
waiver of the notice which is filed with the records of Members’ meetings, or is present at the
meeting in person or by proxy without objecting to the lack of notice. At a meeting of Members,
the presence in person or by proxy of all of the Members constitutes a quorum. Telephonic or
video conference participation in any meeting by any Member shall constitute such Member’s
presence at such meeting for all purposes of this Agreement. A Member may vote either in person
or by written proxy signed by the Member or by the Member’s duly authorized
attorney in fact.

               Section 7.4. Intentionally Omitted.

                Section 7.5. Indemnification: The Company shall defend, indemnify and hold
harmless the Manager and each of the Members from and against all costs, expenses (including,
without limitation, reasonable attorneys’ fees and disbursements), liabilities, claims, demands,
actions, suits and proceedings arising out of or incidental to any act performed or omitted to be
performed by the Manager or such Member in connection with the business of the Company,
provided that such act or omission was taken or made in good faith, was reasonably believed by
the Manager or such Member too be in the best interests of the Company and within the scope of
00311432.4

===== PAGE 250 =====
authority granted or reserved to the Manager or such Member under this Agreement, and did not
constitute fraud, bad faith, willful misconduct or gross negligence on the part of the Manager or
such Member and provided, further, that the indemnity under this Section 7.4 shall be paid solely
out of and to the extent of Company assets and shall not be a personal obligation of any Member.

                Section 7.6. Liability of Manager: Notwithstanding anything to the contrary set
forth in this Agreement, Manager shall not be liable for losses sustained or liabilities incurred as a
result of errors in judgment or of any act or omission, if Manager acted in good faith and in
accordance with the provisions of this Agreement, and was not a result of Manager’s gross
negligence or willful misconduct or fraud.

                Section 7.7. Title to Company Assets: Title to Company assets, whether real,
personal or mixed and whether tangible or intangible, shall be deemed to be owned by the
Company as an entity, and no Member, individually or collectively, shall have any ownership
interest in such Company assets or any portion thereof. Title to any or all of the Company assets
shall be held in the name of the Company.

               Section 7.8. Other Business Ventures: The Members and their respective
Affiliates may engage in or possess an interest in any other business ventures, independently or
with others, and neither the Company nor the other Members shall have any right by virtue of this
Agreement in or to such independent ventured or the income or profits derived therefrom.

                Section 7.9. Manager Expenses and Fees:         Except as specified in this
Agreement, no Manager or Affiliate of any Manager is entitled to remuneration for services
rendered or goods provided to the Company. The Manager and his Affiliates shall receive only the
fees as set forth in this agreement.

                                  ARTICLE 8
                   BOOKS, RECORDS, ACCOUNTING AND REPORTS

                Section 8.1. Records and Accounting: Manager shall keep or cause to be kept
at the principal office of the Company complete and accurate books and records, and copies of all
tax returns and leases for the Property and any other material agreements relating to the Property
and those records and documents required to be maintained by the Act and other books and records
deemed by Manager to be appropriate with respect to the Company’s business. Any records
maintained by or on behalf of the Company in the regular course of its business may be kept
electronically on an information storage device, provided that the records so maintained are
convertible into clearly legible written form within a reasonable period of time. The books of the
Company shall be maintained, for financial reporting purposes, on an accrual basis in accordance
with generally accepted accounting principles, or such other basis as Manager determines to be
necessary or appropriate, with the approval of the Members, which shall not be unreasonably
withheld. All Members shall have the right on reasonable notice to inspect (and make copies of)
the Company records during normal business hours.

               Section 8.2. Fiscal Year: The fiscal year of the Company for reporting
requirements, budgeting, financial statement purposes and all other purposes shall be the calendar
year. The fiscal year for income tax purposes shall be as stated in the Code.

              Section 8.3. Reports: As soon as practicable, but in no event later than one
hundred twenty (120) days after the close of each Company Year, the Manager shall cause to be
prepared as of the close of the Company Year, an annual report containing financial statements of
the Company for such Company Year.

                                          ARTICLE 9
                                         TAX MATTERS

                Section 9.1. Preparation of Tax Returns:               The Tax Matters Member
shall arrange for the preparation and timely filing of all returns of Company, income, gains,
deductions, losses and other items required of the Company for federal and state income tax
purposes and shall furnish, within thirty (30) days prior to the return filing date applicable to each
taxable year (including any permitted extension dates), the tax information reasonably required by
the Members for federal and state income tax reporting purposes.

              Section 9.2. Tax Elections:           The Tax Matters Member shall determine
whether to make any available election pursuant to the Code, and shall have the right to seek to
revoke any tax election it makes upon the Members’ determination that such revocation is in the
00311432.4

===== PAGE 252 =====
best interests of the Members.

               Section 9.3.      Tax Matters Member:

                            A.    The Manager shall be the “Tax Matters Member” of the
Company for federal income tax purposes.

                              B.     The Tax Matters Member shall receive no compensation for
its services as Tax Matters Member. All reasonable out-of-pocket third party costs and expenses
incurred by the Tax Matters Member in performing its duties as such (including reasonable legal
and accounting fees and expenses) shall be borne by the Company. Nothing herein shall be
construed to restrict the Company from engaging an accounting firm approved by all Members to
assist the Tax Matters Member in discharging its duties hereunder, so long as the compensation
paid by the Company for such services is reasonable.

                               C.     The Tax Matters Member shall provide a draft of each
income tax return to be filed on behalf of the Company to the other Members at least ten (10) days
prior to the filing of each return, and the other Members shall be afforded the opportunity to
comment on each such tax return if it so desires.

                                 ARTICLE 10
              SALE OF PROPERTY; TRANSFER OF COMPANY INTEREST

         Section 10.1. Limitations on Transferability: No Member shall directly or indirectly
sell, assign, pledge, hypothecate or otherwise dispose of its interest in the Company or any part
thereof (a “Transfer”) or retire or withdraw from the Company, without the prior written consent
of the Manager, except as provided in this Article 10. Any actual, purported or attempted direct
or indirect transfer by any Member of all or any part of its interest in the Company or the Property
which does not comply with the provisions of this Article 10 shall be void and of no effect.
Notwithstanding the foregoing, each Member shall be entitled, without the consent of the other
Members, to transfer its Company Interest to any Permitted Transferee of such Member. Any
Permitted Transferee and any transferee pursuant to an Involuntary Transfer shall automatically
be admitted as an Economic Interest Holder with no rights to vote on any matters coming before
the Members, and, shall not be admitted as a Member unless the conditions of Section 10.4 are
satisfied. Any Person to whom Company Interests are attempted to be transferred in violation of
this Section 10.1 shall not be entitled to vote on matters coming before the Members, participate
in the management of the Company, act as an agent of the Company, receive distributions from
the Company, or have any other rights in or with respect to the Company Interests

         Section 10.2. Right of First Transfer Offer and Additional Purchase Right:

                       10.2.1. Right of First Transfer Offer.

                         (1)  If, after the fifth (5th) anniversary of the date hereof, a Member
desires to Transfer its Company Interests to any Person other than a Permitted Transferee (such
selling Member, the “Transfer Selling Member”) then, the Transfer Selling Member shall send a
written notice (the “Proposed Sale Notice”) to the Manager to the effect that the Transfer Selling
Member intends to sell its Company Interest to a third party at a specified price (such price the
00311432.4

===== PAGE 253 =====
“Intended Sale Price”) and stating all of the other material terms and conditions which the Transfer
Selling Member would accept with respect to such sale (the “Material Sale Conditions”), including
timing of closing, deposit amount, method of payment and financing, if any. The Members shall
have the option to acquire the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interests, on a pro rate basis
to the members electing to purchase said interests, (such option, whether by Manager, a Transfer
Purchasing Member or the Company, as applicable pursuant to the provisions of Subsection 10.2.1,
referred to as a "Right of First Offer") by accepting the terms and conditions of the Proposed Sales
Notice in a notice (an “Acceptance Notice”) to the Transfer Selling Member given within ten (10)
days of receipt of the Proposed Sale Notice, that the Members elect to purchase the Transfer Selling
Member’s Company Interests. If so elected, the Members shall acquire the Company Interest of
the Transfer Selling Member in accordance with the terms and provisions of the Proposed Sale
Notice, on a pro rata basis to the members electing to purchase said interests.

                         (2)   Intentionally omitted

                           (3)   All or any of the Non-Selling Members shall have the right to
acquire the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest at the Intended Sale Price and upon the
Material Sale Conditions, provided that such Right of First Offer shall be subject to the
limitations set forth in this Subsection 10.2.1(3). Such Right of First Offer must be exercised by
the Non-Selling Member(s) within ten (10) days after giving the Proposed Sale Notice, by an
Acceptance Notice from the Non-Selling Member(s) wishing to exercise its Right of First Offer
indicating that it elects to exercise such First Purchase Right and simultaneously therewith the
Member(s) exercising such Right of First Offer (such Member so exercising the First Purchase
Right, is referred to herein as the “Transfer Purchasing Member”) shall forward to the party
designated as the escrow agent who would receive any deposit under the Material Sale
Conditions the amount of the deposit required to be paid at the time of the execution of any
contract of sale with the Transfer Selling Member, provided that, notwithstanding such Material
Sale Conditions, such deposit by such Transfer Purchasing Member(s) shall not exceed five
percent (5%) of the Intended Sale Price (failure to make such deposit if so required by Material
Sale Conditions shall render the Transfer Purchasing Member’s Acceptance Notice ineffective).
Time is of the essence with respect to the giving of such Acceptance Notice and accordingly,
failure to give an Acceptance Notice within the ten (10) day period shall be deemed to be a
waiver by the Non-Selling Member to exercise the Right of First Offer with respect to the
Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interests as provided for in Proposed Sale Notice. In the
event there is more than one Transfer Purchasing Member and such Transfer Purchasing
Members do not agree on the allocation of the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest,
then, in such event, the Company Interest of the Transfer Selling Member shall be divided in
proportion with the Percentages Interests then held by the Transfer Purchasing Members.

                         (4)   If any Non-Selling Member, as the Transfer Purchasing Member,
timely exercises its Right of First Offer, then such Transfer Purchasing Member, or any designee
of the Transfer Purchasing Member, shall be obligated to acquire the Transfer Selling Member’s
Company Interests for the Intended Sale Price consistent with the Material Sale Conditions and
the Transfer Selling Member shall be required to Transfer such Transfer Selling Member’s
Company Interest to the Transfer Purchasing Member, or any designees of such Transfer
Purchasing Member, for such Intended Sale Price upon such Material Sale Conditions. If all the
Non-Selling Members (y) affirmatively elect, by notice to the Transfer Selling Member, not to
exercise the Right of First Offer or (z) fail to timely exercise the Right of First Offer as aforesaid,
then in either case, the Transfer Selling Member shall send the Manager a notice thereof, along
00311432.4

===== PAGE 254 =====
with an additional Proposed Sale Notice, offering to sell the Transfer Selling Member’s Company
Interests to the Company. In the event that, within ten (10) days after receipt of such Proposed Sale
Notice the Members holding a majority of the Company’s Interests, excluding the Transfer Selling
Member’s Company Interests, elect to have the Company purchase the Transfer Selling Member’s
Company Interests, the Manager shall send an Acceptance Notice to the Transfer Selling Member,
in which event the Company shall be obligated to acquire the Transfer Selling Member’s Company
Interests for the Intended Sale Price consistent with the Material Sale Conditions and the Transfer
Selling Member shall be required to Transfer such Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest
to the Company, for such Intended Sale Price upon such Material Sale Conditions.

                         (5)    If the Company (y) affirmatively elects, by notice to the Transfer
Selling Member, not to exercise the Right of First Offer or (z) fails to timely exercise the Right of
First Offer as aforesaid, then in either case, the Transfer Selling Member shall be free to Transfer
the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest to a third party reasonably approved by the
Manager, at the Intended Sale Price upon the Material Sale Conditions (but otherwise subject to
the provisions of this Agreement), the closing of which must occur within a period (the “Free
Member Sale Period”) one hundred eighty (180) days after the date of the Company fails to timely
exercise the Right of First Offer. If the Transfer Selling Member Transfers its Company Interests
pursuant to this Subsection 10.2.1(5), such transferee, subject to the terms of Section 10.4, shall
be merely an Economic Interest Holder and not a Member. If such Transfer by the Transfer Selling
Member, as outlined in the Proposed Sale Notice, does not occur within such one hundred eighty
(180) day period, or if such Transfer by the Transfer Selling Member is for a price which is less
than ninety-five (95%) percent of the Intended Sale Price, or if such Transfer is to be otherwise
materially inconsistent with the Material Sale Conditions, then, (i) such Transfer shall be null and
void ab initio, and (ii) notwithstanding any prior waiver or declaration by the Manager, Non-
Selling Member or the Company to the contrary, the Right of First Offer for the benefit of the
Manager, Non-Selling Member and the Company shall be deemed to be reinstated and shall again
apply to any Transfer of the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest to any third party. At
the request of the Manager, the Transfer Selling Member shall promptly provide written reports as
to the status of the proposed Transfer as outlined in the Proposed Sale Notice.

                          (6)   The Right of First Offer as contained herein shall continue to remain
in effect with respect to any subsequent Transfers of the Company Interest which were the subject
of the Proposed Sale Notice, whether or not a sale to the third party by the Transfer Selling Member
is consummated, except that if the Transfer Purchasing Member defaults in taking title thereto and
paying the Intended Sale Price as provided above, then the deposit paid by such Transfer
Purchasing Member shall be forfeited and paid to the Transfer Selling Member, as agreed
liquidated damages as the sole remedy of the Transfer Selling Member, and the Right of First Offer
granted to such Transfer Purchasing Member under this Subsection 10.2.1 shall thereupon be and
become null and void and thereafter such Transfer Purchasing Member shall no longer at any time
be entitled to exercise the Right of First Offer set forth in this Subsection 10.2.1 with respect to
the Transfer of the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest.




00311432.4

===== PAGE 255 =====
                        10.2.2. Receipt of Bona Fide Offer – Additional First Purchase Right.

                         (1) In addition to the Right of First Offer under Section 10.2.1, but subject
to the limitations set forth in Subsection 10.2.2(2), if at any time after the fifth (5th) anniversary of
the date hereof a Member receives a bona fide written offer from any third party (each such third
party is referred to herein as an “Offeror”) to purchase its Company Interest (an “Offer”), which
such Member intends to accept, then such Member shall be deemed a Transfer Selling Member as
described in Subsection 10.2.1 hereof and such Transfer Selling Member shall give the Manager
(and the Non-Selling Members and Company, as applicable) a Proposed Sale Notice (as described
in, and pursuant to, Subsection 10.2.1 hereof) within five (5) Business Days after it has reached an
agreement in principal as to such Offer with respect to the Intended Sale Price and the Material
Sale Conditions. Thereupon, the Manager, Non-Selling Members or Company, as applicable, shall
have the option and right to purchase the Transfer Selling Member’s Company Interest in
accordance with the Intended Sale Price and the Material Sale Conditions, as outlined in the
Proposed Sale Notice, as if same were the Right of First Offer under the provisions of Subsection
10.2.1 hereof (including the priority of rights set forth therein), except that each of the Manager’s,
Non-Selling Members’ and Company’s, as applicable, time to give an Acceptance Notice shall be
ten (10) Business Days rather than the ten (10) days set forth in Subsection 10.2.1 hereof. Subject
to the aforesaid provisions of this Subsection 10.2.2, all of the other provisions of Subsection
10.2.1 hereof, including the giving, and five (5%) limit, of a deposit under Subsection 10.2.1(3),
shall apply to such accepted Offer as if such Offer was the Intended Sale Price and the Material
Sale Conditions, except that if the Transfer Selling Member and Offeror thereafter negotiate any
material change to the terms and conditions contained in the original Offer (including a price
reduction greater than five (5%) percent of the Intended Sale Price as contained in such Offer),
then the Transfer Selling Member shall again deliver to the Manager, Non-Selling Members and
Company, as applicable, a new Proposed Sale Notice with respect to such Offer as so modified
(the “Modified Offer”), and the Manager, Non-Selling Members and Company, as applicable, shall
have the right and option to either accept or reject the Modified Offer within seven (7) Business
Days after receipt of the new Proposed Sale Notice with respect to such Modified Offer, and if the
Manager, Non-Selling Members and Company, as applicable, fail to deliver a new Acceptance
Notice within such seven (7) Business Day period accepting the terms of such Modified Offer,
the Manager, Non- Selling Member and Company, as applicable, shall be deemed to have rejected
the Modified Offer. If the Manager, Non-Selling Members, and Company, by written notice to the
Transfer Selling Member, reject the Modified Offer, then the Transfer Selling Member thereafter
shall be free to consummate a sale of its Company Interest to the Offeror upon the price, terms and
conditions contained in the Modified Offer; provided, however, if a sale of the Transfer Selling
Member’s Company Interest to the Offeror is not consummated within ninety (90) days following
the end of the aforesaid ten (10) or seven (7) Business Days period, as applicable, then the Right
of First Offer set forth in Subsection 10.2.1 hereof and the additional first purchase right set forth
in this Subsection 10.2.2 shall again be applicable to a sale of such Transfer Selling Member’s
Company Interest.

                       10.2.3. Third Party Agreement – Transfer Prohibition. Notwithstanding
anything to the contrary contained in this Section 10.2.3, the Members shall not be entitled to make
any Transfer, whether to a Permitted Transferee or otherwise, if such Transfer would be in
violation of any agreements to which the Company is a party (including, without limitation, any


00311432.4

===== PAGE 256 =====
loan documents) unless the other parties to such agreements consent to, or waive any prohibition
of, such Transfer. Upon the request of any Member, the Company shall promptly certify to such
Member whether it is the Company’s position that a proposed Transfer would be in violation of
any such agreements.

                Section 10.3. Additional Members:            The Manager may cause the
Company to transfer and sell Company Interests to third party investors, and, subject to compliance
with Section 10.4, admit third party investors to the Company as Members (each, an "Additional
Member"). In the event such Company Interests are issued, other than as provided in Section 4.2,
the Company Interests of Members who have not defaulted on their obligations hereunder shall
not be diluted by the interest of an Additional Member. Until such time as the conditions of Section
10.4 are satisfied, an Additional Member shall admitted as an Economic Interest Holder with no
rights to vote on any matters coming before the Members. This Operating Agreement shall be
amended upon the admittance of an Additional Member to the extent required to reflect the
additional membership. No member shall unreasonably object to any amendment to the Operating
Agreement in connection with the admittance of an Additional Member.

                Section 10.4. Admittance of Additional Members: No Additional Member in
accordance with Section 10.3 hereof or a Permitted Transferee or a Transferee pursuant to Section
10.2 hereof shall be admitted to the Company as a Member unless (i) the Manager consents in
writing, (ii) the Additional Member or transferee, as applicable, agrees in writing, in form and
substance reasonably satisfactory to Manager, to (x) assume and be bound by all the obligations
that apply to the Additional Member’s or transferee’s Company Interest which accrue after the
transfer is made, (y) irrevocably consent to the jurisdiction of the courts of the State of Rhode
Island and of the United States of America located therein with respect to any matter arising under
this Agreement, and (z) be subject to all the restrictions to which the other Members are subject
under the terms of this Agreement; and (iii) all required consents of mortgagees and other material
creditors of the Company, if any, to such transfer shall have been obtained in writing and delivered
to Manager. Upon the satisfaction of the foregoing requirements, the Additional Member,
transferee or Permitted Transferee, as applicable, shall be admitted to the Company as a Member,
provided, however, if any individual Permitted Transferee is less than twenty five (25) years
of age, then such Permitted Transferee shall become only an unadmitted assignee of a Member as
an Economic Interest Holder, and shall not become a Member until such Permitted Transferee,
trustee or executor attains twenty five (25) years of age.

                Section 10.5. Dispute Resolution. (a) The parties hereto agree that all problems or
claims arising out of or in connection with this Agreement and all other agreements or other
instruments executed in connection herewith, including any claim for specific performance
(collectively “Disputes”), the Members shall, in good faith, use their reasonable best efforts to
resolve the Dispute. If after such efforts the Members are unable within ten (10) days of the arising
of the Dispute to resolve the Dispute in good faith, any party may submit the dispute to a an
arbitration panel for resolution. In the event the parties are unable to agree upon an arbitration
panel, each of the parties to the dispute shall select one arbitrator, and such two arbitrators shall
select a third arbitrator, whom together shall constitute the panel. The fees and expenses of such
dispute resolution shall be borne by the non-prevailing party, as determined by such arbitration.

(b) Notwithstanding the foregoing, in the event of a dispute between a Manager and any Non-
Managing-Member, rather than submit to arbitration the Managers may elect to have the Company
to acquire such Non-Managing-Member’s Units for fair market value, as determined by a third
00311432.4

===== PAGE 257 =====
party appraiser with experience valuing businesses such as the Company. For avoidance of doubt,
the provisions of this Section shall apply upon the making or filing of any claim by a Non-
Managing-Member against a Manager.

                                    ARTICLE 11
                    DISSOLUTION, LIQUIDATION AND TERMINATION

               Section 11.1. Dissolution: A.          The Company shall dissolve, and its affairs
 shall be wound up, only upon the first to occur of any of the following:

                                 (i)     The sale, taking in condemnation or by eminent domain or
 other disposition of all or substantially all of the assets and properties of the Company;

                                   (ii)    If the Manager shall (i) make a general assignment for the
 benefit of its creditors, (ii) generally not pay its debts as they become due, (iii) admit in writing its
 inability to pay its debts as they mature, (iv) commence any case, proceeding or other action
 seeking reorganization, arrangement, adjustment, liquidation, dissolution or composition of it or
 its debts under any law relating to bankruptcy, insolvency, reorganization or relief of debtors, or
 seeking appointment of a receiver, trustee, custodian or other similar official for it or for all or any
 substantial part of its property; or, if any case, proceeding or other action against the Manager
 shall be commenced seeking to have an order for relief entered against it as debtor, or seeking
 reorganization, arrangement, adjustment, liquidation, dissolution or composition of it or its debts
 under any law relating to bankruptcy, insolvency, reorganization or relief of debtors, or seeking
 appointment of a receiver, trustee, custodian or other similar official for it or for all or any
 substantial part of its property, and such case, proceeding or other action results in the entry of an
 order for relief against it which is not dismissed within ninety (90) days; provided, that the majority
 of the remaining Members may elect to continue the Company;

                                 (iii)   Upon the entry of a decree of judicial dissolution under the
 Act.

                                 (iv)    Upon the written agreement of all of the Members.

                 Section 11.2. Winding-Up: Upon any dissolution of the Company in accordance
 with the provisions of Section 11.1, Manager (or, in the event such dissolution has occurred by
 reason of the occurrence to Manager of an event specified in Section 11.1, the Members), on behalf
 of the Members, shall wind up the business of the Company, sell, lease, exchange, transfer or
 otherwise dispose of any or all Company assets within a reasonable time, pay or arrange for the
 payment of all Company debts and expenses (including debts to the Members, if any) and, after
 crediting or debiting each Economic Interest Holder’s Capital Account with its allocable share of
 Net Income and Net Loss and other items in accordance with Article 5 hereof, shall distribute the
 balance of the Company assets among the Economic Interest Holders in accordance with Section
 6.1B.

                                       ARTICLE 13
                                   GENERAL PROVISIONS

                Section 13.1 Addresses and Notice:             Any notice, request, demand,
statement, authorization, approval or consent made hereunder shall be in writing and shall be hand
delivered or sent by Federal Express, or other reputable national courier service, or by postage
prepaid registered or certified mail, return receipt requested, and shall be deemed given
(i) when received or when delivery is refused at the following addresses if hand delivered or sent
by Federal Express, or other reputable national courier service, and (ii) three (3) business days
after being postmarked if sent by registered or certified mail, return receipt requested. All notices
shall be sent to the address set forth for each Member on Schedule A, with a copy to
__________________. Each party may designate a change of address by notice to the other parties,
given at least fifteen (15) days before such change of address is to become effective.

                Section 13.2. Titles and Captions: All article or section titles or captions in this
Agreement are for convenience only. They shall not be deemed part of this Agreement and in no
way define, limit, extend or described the scope or intent of any provisions hereof. Except as
specifically provided otherwise, references to “Articles” and “Sections” are to Articles and
Sections of this Agreement.

               Section 13.3. Pronouns and Plurals: Whenever the context may require, any
pronoun used in this Agreement shall include the corresponding masculine, feminine or neuter
forms, and the singular form of nouns, pronouns and verbs shall include the plural and vice versa.




00311432.4

===== PAGE 260 =====
                Section 13.4. Further Action: The parties shall execute and deliver all documents,
provided all information and take or refrain from taking action as may be necessary or appropriate
to achieve the purposes of this Agreement.

                Section 13.5. Binding Effect: This Agreement shall be binding upon and inure
to the benefit of the parties hereto and their respective heirs, executors, administrators, successors,
legal representatives and permitted assigns.

              Section 13.6. Amendments:          This Agreement may not                 be amended
without the unanimous written approval of the Members.

                Section 13.7. Creditors: None of the provisions of this Agreement shall be for
the benefit of, or shall be enforceable by, any creditor of the Company.

              Section 13.8. Waiver: No failure by any party to insist upon the strict performance
of any covenant, duty, agreement or condition of this Agreement or to exercise any right or remedy
consequent upon a breach hereof shall constitute waiver of any such breach or any other covenant,
duty, agreement or condition.

                Section 13.9. Counterparts:            This Agreement may be executed in
counterparts, all of which together shall constitute one agreement binding on all of the parties
hereto, notwithstanding that all such parties are not signatories to the original or the same
counterpart. Each party shall become bound by this Agreement when the same is executed and
delivered by all parties hereto. Signatures delivered by facsimile transmission or by e-mail delivery
of a “.pdf” format data file, shall be given the same legal force and effect as original signatures.

                Section 13.10. Applicable Law: This Agreement shall be construed and enforced
in accordance with and governed by the laws of the State of Rhode Island, without regard to the
principles of conflicts of laws thereof.

               Section 13.11. Invalidity of Provisions: If any provision of this Agreement is or
becomes invalid, illegal or unenforceable in any respect, the validity, legality and enforceability
of the remaining provisions contained herein shall not be affected thereby.

              Section 13.12. Entire Agreement: This Agreement contains the entire
understanding and agreement among the Members with respect to the subject matter hereof and
supersedes any other prior written or oral understandings or agreements among them with respect
thereto.

               Section 13.13. Rights and Remedies:          The parties hereto shall have all
rights and remedies set forth in this Agreement and available at law or in equity. The rights and
remedies of any of the parties hereunder shall not be mutually exclusive, and the exercise of one
or more rights or remedies provided herein at law or in equity shall not preclude the exercise of
any other right or remedy provided herein at law or in equity. Each of the parties confirms that
damages at law may be an inadequate remedy for a breach or threatened breach of any provision
hereof. The respective rights and obligations hereunder shall be enforceable by specific
performance, injunction or other suitable remedy, but nothing herein contained is intended to or


00311432.4

===== PAGE 261 =====
shall limit or affect any rights of law, or by statute or otherwise of any party aggrieved as against
the other party for a breach or threatened breach of any provision hereof, it being the intention by
this paragraph to make clear the agreement of the parties that the respective rights and obligations
of the parties hereunder shall be enforceable in equity as well as at law or otherwise.

               Section 13.14. Brokerage: Each Member represents that it dealt with no broker
in connection with the execution and delivery of this Agreement and consummation of the
transactions contemplated hereby.

                 Section 13.15. Limitation of Liability: Notwithstanding anything to the contrary
contained in this Agreement, no recourse shall be had for the payment of any loans or other
payments due or for any other claim under this Agreement or based on the failure of performance
or observance of any of the terms and conditions of this Agreement against any Member, any
affiliate of any Member of any principal, partner, member, shareholder, controlling person, officer,
director, agent or employee of any of the aforesaid Persons or any of their respective assets other
than such Member’s interest in the Company or assets of the Company to which such Member is
entitled under any rule of law, statute or constitution, or by the enforcement of any assessment or
penalty, or otherwise, nor shall any of such Persons be personally liable for any contributions,
loans, payments or claims, or personally liable for any deficiency judgment based thereon or with
respect thereto, it being expressly understood that the sole remedies of the Company or any other
Member with respect to such amounts and claims shall be against such interest in the Company
and the assets of the Company to which such Member is entitled and as otherwise expressly set
forth in this Agreement, and that all such liability of the aforesaid Persons, except as expressly
provided in this Section, is expressly waived and released as a condition of, and as consideration
for, the execution of this Agreement and the admission of each Member to the Company; provided,
however, that nothing contained in this Agreement (including, without limitation, the provisions
of this Section), (a) shall constitute a waiver of any obligation of a Member under this Agreement,
(b) shall be taken to prevent recourse to and the enforcement against such Company interest and
the assets of the Company to which such Member is entitled for all of the respective liabilities,
obligations and undertakings of the aforesaid Persons contained in this Agreement, (c) shall be
taken to prevent recourse to and the enforcement against (i) a transferring Member of its liabilities,
obligations and undertakings contained in any instrument of assignment or indemnity delivered in
connection with such transfer (but such recourse shall be limited to the proceeds received by such
transferring Member in connection with such assignment or transfer) or (ii) any security delivered
by any of the aforesaid Persons pursuant to this Agreement, or (d) shall be taken to limit or restrict
any action or proceeding against any of the aforesaid Persons which does not seek damages or a
money judgment or does not seek to compel payment of money (or the performance of obligations
which would require the payment of money) by any of the aforesaid Persons.

              Section 13.16. Continuation of the Company: Any rule of law to the contrary
notwithstanding, except as specifically provided in this Agreement:

                       (a)     The Members shall continue as Members hereunder;

waive their right to, seek, bring or maintain any action in any court of law or equity for partition
of the Company, or any of its assets, except as specifically provided for herein.

                 Section 13.17. Disposition of Documents: Copies of all documents and records of
the Company, including, without limitation, all Leases, financial records and bank statements shall
be available in the Rhode Island metropolitan area to all Members upon the termination of the
Company. Upon the request of any Member, copies of all such documents and records shall be
provided to such Member. The Member holding such documents and records shall retain them for
a period of at least six (6) years after the termination of the Company and shall make copies thereof
available to any other Member during such period.

ISLAND COUNTY AND THE UNITED STATES DISTRICT COURT FOR THE SOUTHERN

SUIT OR PROCEEDING.

         Section 13.19 OFAC REPRESENTATION: Each Member represents and warrants
that it is not listed, nor is it owned or controlled by, or acting for or on behalf of any person or
entity, on the list of Specially Designated Nationals and Blocked Persons maintained by the Office
of Foreign Assets Control of the United States Department of the Treasury, or any other list of
persons or entities with whom Manager and/or the Company is restricted from doing business with
(“OFAC List”). Each Member shall indemnify and hold the Manager, the Company and the other
Members (collectively the “Indemnified Parties”) harmless from and against all losses, damages,
liabilities, cost and expenses (including, without limitation, reasonable attorneys' fees and
expenses) that are incurred from a proceeding or claim made by a third party against an
Indemnified Party arising or alleged to arise from a misrepresentation made under this Section
13.19.

OPERATING AGREEMENT
OF
GRI SNF INVESTORS LLC

This Operating Agreement (this “Agreement”) of GRI SNF INVESTORS LLC
(the “Company”), is entered into this 29° day of October, 2020, by the undersigned (the
“Members”).

WHEREAS, the Company was formed as a Delaware limited liability company,
pursuant to the Delaware Limited Liability Company Act, as amended from time to time
(the “Act”); and

WHEREAS, the Members desire to enter into this Agreement to define formally
and express the terms of the Company and its rights and obligations with respect thereto.

NOW, THEREFORE, in consideration of the agreements and obligations set forth
herein and for other good and valuable consideration, the Members hereby agree as
follows:

1. Formation. The Company has been formed and established as a Delaware
limited liability company by the filing of the Certificate of Formation, pursuant to the Act
(the “Articles”) with the Secretary of State of the State of Delaware. The Members hereby
ratify, confirm and approve in all respects the actions taken in organizing the Company,
including, without limitation, the preparation and filing with the Secretary of State of the
State of Delaware of the Articles and any additional filings, amendments and/or
restatements thereof necessary with respect to qualification of the Company to do business.

2. Name. The name of the limited liability company pursuant to the Articles is
“GRI SNF Investors LLC”.

3. Purpose. The purpose of the Company is to engage in any business in
which limited liability companies may be involved, under the Act.

4. Principal Office and Place of Business. The principal office of the
Company shall be located at such place that the Manager (as defined herein) may select
from time to time.

5. Registered Agent. The name and address of the registered agent of the
Company are set forth in the organizational documents of the Company.

6. Members and Capital Contribution. The name of the Members and the
amount of cash or other property contributed or to be contributed by the Members to the
capital of the Company are set forth on Schedule A attached hereto and shall be listed on
the books and records of the Company. The Manager of the Company shall be required to
update the books and records, and the aforementioned Schedule, from time to time as
necessary to accurately reflect the information therein.

{1223/023/00440159.2}

===== PAGE 268 =====
[OCR]
7. Additional Capital Contributions. The Members shall not be required to
make any additional contributions of capital to the Company, although the Members may
from time to time agree to make additional contributions to the Company.

8. Management of the Company. Dov Green shall serve as "Manager” of the
Company (the “Manager”), until such party’s successors shall have been duly appointed or
until their earlier resignation or removal. The business and affairs of the Company shall be
managed by the Manager. The Manager shall have the power to do any and all acts
necessary or convenient to or for the furtherance of the purposes described herein,
including all powers, statutory or otherwise, possessed by managers of a limited liability
company under the laws of the State of Delaware. For purposes of administrative
convenience, and in the interest of reducing cumbersome documentation, in the event the
Manager is an entity, such entity may indicate its consent to any action of the Company by
the signature of a manager of such entity as “Authorized Representative” or “Manager”,
which signature shall be deemed for all purposes hereunder to be the duly authorized
signature of the Manager.

9. Dissolution. The Company shall dissolve, and its affairs shall be wound up,
upon the first to occur of the following: (a) the written consent of the Members, or (b) the
entry of a decree of judicial dissolution.

10. Allocation of Profits and Losses. The Company’s profits and losses shall be
allocated to the Members, pro rata in accordance with its ownership of the Company.

11. Distributions. Distributions shall be made to the Members, pro rata in
accordance with its ownership of the Company, at the times and in the aggregate amounts
determined by the Manager.

12. Liability of Members; Manager. Neither the Members nor the Manager
shall have any liability for the obligations or liabilities of the Company except to the extent
provided herein or in the Act.

13. Indemnification. The Company shall indemnify and hold harmless the
Manager and the Members and its partners, shareholders, officers, directors, managers,
employees, agents and representatives and the partners, shareholders, officers, directors,
managers, employees, agents and representatives of such persons to the fullest extent
permitted by the Act.

14. | Amendment. This Agreement may be amended from time to time with the
consent of the Manager.

15. Governing Law. This Agreement shall be governed by, and construed in
accordance with, the laws of the State of Delaware.

is GREEN TREE HEALTHCARE MANAGEMENT LLC.

assigning, transferring, exchanging and otherwise dealing with certain nursing facilities
